She initially coped by tightening her schedules and journaling more, but over several months she found it increasingly hard to maintain the precision and consistency she prided herself on, leading to subtle mistakes with color formulations and missed inventory tasks that drew criticism from her manager. As this persisted over the following year and a half, she started avoiding shifts, withdrawing from the hiking club and senior‑center volunteering, and letting her dream of opening a boutique salon feel unattainable, which deeply conflicted with her identity as reliable and orderly. The turning point came when she arrived late to work multiple times in one month, broke down in the back room stating she \"couldn't trust her own mind anymore,\" and her supervisor, concerned about the marked decline in her functioning and focus, urged her to see a mental health professional, leading to psychiatric evaluation.\n- Chief Complaint: “Lately I can’t think clearly or concentrate the way I used to at work, and it feels like my mind just won’t stay organized.”\n▸ Patient-specific Symptom Manifestations\n- S019 (Delusions): I keep thinking my manager and some of the clients are secretly recording me and sharing it in some group chat to laugh about how I'm messing up colors, and no matter how many times people tell me that's not happening, I just know they're hiding something from me.\n- S020 (Hallucinations): At the salon, especially when I'm mixing dye at the back counter, I hear this man's voice right behind me saying things like, 'You’re ruining her hair' or 'They know you’re a fraud,' even when no one is there and the radio is the only thing actually playing.\n- S023 (Negative_Symptoms): Most days now I just sit on my bed in my work clothes staring at the wall, feeling like I can’t move myself to shower, answer texts, or even turn on a show, and even when my mom calls or friends invite me out, I just feel blank and can’t bring myself to care.\n- S024 (Depressed_Mood): It feels like there’s this heavy gray cloud sitting on my chest all the time, and I wake up already feeling sad and empty, like there’s no point to the day before it’s even started.\n- S026 (Weight_Appetite_Change): I used to love grabbing burritos with my coworkers after a late shift, but now most days I have to force down a piece of toast or a granola bar because my stomach just turns at the thought of food and I’ve watched my clothes get looser without even trying.\n- S028 (Psychomotor_Changes): My coworkers joke that I move like I’m underwater now, but it’s true—just walking from my station to the shampoo bowl or lifting my arms to blow‑dry someone’s hair feels like I’m dragging a hundred pounds and even my speech comes out slow, like my mouth can’t keep up.\n- S030 (Worthlessness_Guilt): I constantly replay every tiny mistake I’ve ever made on a client, like using the wrong toner once last year, and I end up convinced I don’t deserve this job or my friends and that I’ve somehow ruined everyone’s life around me just by existing.\n- S031 (Diminished_Thinking): When I’m trying to plan my appointments or mix formulas now, my brain just fogs over so badly that I’ll reread the same line in the book five times, forget what the client just told me about their hair history, and stand there frozen because I can’t decide something as simple as which developer to grab even though I’ve done it a thousand times before.\n- S032 (Death_Suicide): There are nights when I lie in bed staring at the ceiling thinking it would be better if I just didn’t wake up, and I’ve caught myself picturing how easy it would be to just step in front of a train or take all my meds at once, even though actually doing it still scares me.\n▸ Additional Diagnostic Requirements\n- psychotic_symptoms_and_depressive_episode_occur_concurrently: When the voice started criticizing me at the salon and I became convinced people were recording me, I was already in that heavy, sad fog where getting out of bed felt pointless and I walked around with this constant gray cloud over me.\n- depressive_episode_is_present_for_the_majority_of_the_total_duration_of_the_active_and_residual_portions_of_the_illness_(Criterion_C): Looking back over this whole stretch since things first went downhill at the salon, I can’t really remember more than a few brief weeks at a time when I didn’t feel deeply down, empty, and guilty almost every single day.\n- delusions_and_hallucinations_occur_for_2_or_more_weeks_in_the_absence_of_a_major_mood_episode_(Criterion_B): There was a stretch last fall where, even though I didn’t feel as crushingly sad and could get through my shifts a bit better, I still kept hearing that man’s voice behind me and was sure my coworkers were secretly filming me and laughing about my mistakes.\n- functional_impairment: Because my mind feels so foggy and distracted by these thoughts and the low mood, I’ve cut my hours at the salon, been written up for mistakes and lateness, stopped seeing friends or going to my hiking group, and some days I don’t even manage basic things like showering or cleaning my room.\n- duration: This has been going on ever since I went from cosmetology school into full-time work at the salon, so for almost two years now it feels like my mind hasn’t gone back to how it used to be.\n- psychotic_symptoms_duration: The voice and the feeling that people are secretly recording and judging me have been happening pretty steadily for about a year and a few months now, ever since that first really rough period at the salon.\n- depressive_episode_duration: I’ve felt consistently low, empty, and weighed down most days for a little over a year now, starting after those first months when work pressure really started to pile up.\n\nRules:\n- If the patient can specifically answer based on the way I used to at work, and it feels like my mind just won’t stay organized.”\n▸ Patient-specific Symptom Manifestations\n- S019 (Delusions): I keep thinking my manager and some of the clients are you’re a fraud,' even when no one is there and the radio is the only thing actually playing.\n- S023 (Negative_Symptoms): Most days now I just sit on my bed in my work clothes staring at the wall, developer to grab even though I’ve done it a thousand times before.\n- S032 (Death_Suicide): There are nights when I lie in bed staring at the ceiling thinking it would be better if I just didn’t wake feeling like I can’t move myself to shower, answer texts, or even turn on a show, and even when my mom calls or friends invite me out, I just feel blank and can’t bring myself to care.\n- S024 Subtypes: Interference by Worry, Inability to Sustain Attention, Mind Going Blank\n[S060] Irritability: A state of reacting sensitively or sharply to minor stimuli. Subtypes: Impulsive Anger, Verbal Aggression, Physical Aggression, Chronic Irritability\n[S078] Reckless_Self_Destructive_Behavior: Reckless or self-destructive behavior pattern that endangers one's safety or health.
